    10	const RETAIN_PER_KIND = 10;
    11	
    12	/** @param {string} root */
    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {
    14	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    15	  return {
    16	    dir,
    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,
    18	    bak: `${dir}/bundle.json.bak`,
    19	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    20	    handoffMd: `${dir}/HANDOFF.md`,
    21	    historyDir: `${dir}/history`,
    22	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    23	    logDir: `${dir}/log`,
    24	  };
    25	}
    26	
    27	/** @param {string} iso */
    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    29	
    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
    39	 */
    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
    43	  try {
    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
    50	        return;
    51	      }
    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
    55	
    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
    60	        }
    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
    63	        }
    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
    66	      } else {
    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
    69	      }
    70	    });
    71	  } catch (err) {
    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
    74	      return;
    75	    }
    76	    throw err;
    77	  }
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
    83	 */
    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
    89	    warnings.push(
    90	      snap.ok
    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
    93	    );
    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
    97	      return bak.value;
    98	    }
    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   103	    }
   104	    return null;
   105	  }
   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}
   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/**
   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
   167	 */
   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   182	    }
   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
   185	  });
   186	}
   187	
   188	/**
   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
   192	 */
   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;
   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
   222	      }
   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
   224	      return seq;
   225	    }),
   226	  );
   227	}
   228	
   229	/**
   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   234	 */
   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
   237	}
   238	
   239	/**
   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   243	 */
   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   253	
   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
   256	
   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   261	    } else {
   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
   263	    }
   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
   266	    } else {
   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
   268	    }
   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
   270	
   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
   272	    const freezes = io.fs
   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
   275	      .sort();
   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
   280	        try {
   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
   282	        } catch {
   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
   284	        }
   285	      }
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
   289	    return stem;
   290	  });

    11	  return `${path}.tmp.${process.pid}-${tmpCounter}`;
    12	}
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * Rename with retry on EPERM (Windows AV/indexer lock pattern): 3 attempts total.
    16	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} from @param {string} to
    17	 */
    18	function renameWithRetry(fs, from, to) {
    19	  let lastErr;
    20	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
    21	    try {
    22	      fs.renameSync(from, to);
    23	      return;
    24	    } catch (err) {
    25	      lastErr = err;
    26	      if (!err || /** @type {any} */ (err).code !== 'EPERM') throw err;
    27	    }
    28	  }
    29	  throw lastErr;
    30	}
    31	
    32	/**
    33	 * Write text atomically: tmp sibling + rename. The live target is never
    34	 * truncated or partially written.
    35	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    36	 */
    37	export function atomicWriteText(fs, path, content) {
    38	  const tmp = tmpPathFor(path);
    39	  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, content);
    40	  try {
    41	    renameWithRetry(fs, tmp, path);
    42	  } catch (err) {
    43	    try {
    44	      fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
    45	    } catch {
    46	      // leftover tmp is harmless; cleanupTmp removes it later
    47	    }
    48	    throw err;
    49	  }
    50	}
    51	
    52	/**
    53	 * Atomically write pretty-printed JSON (2-space, trailing newline).
    54	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    55	 */
    56	export function atomicWriteJson(fs, path, obj) {
    57	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) + '\n');
    58	}
    59	
    60	/**
    61	 * Read + parse JSON without ever throwing.
    62	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    63	 * @returns {{ok: true, value: any} | {ok: false, error: string}}
    64	 */
    65	export function safeReadJson(fs, path) {
    66	  let raw;
    67	  try {
    68	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    69	  } catch (err) {
    70	    return { ok: false, error: `read failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code ?? err}` };
    71	  }
    72	  try {
    73	    return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(raw) };
    74	  } catch (err) {
    75	    return { ok: false, error: `parse failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err}` };
    76	  }
    77	}
    78	
    79	/**
    80	 * Copy the current content to <path>.bak (when present), then write atomically.
    81	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    82	 */
    83	export function backupThenWrite(fs, path, content) {
    84	  if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
    85	    fs.copyFileSync(path, `${path}.bak`);
    86	  }
    87	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, content);
    88	}

    92	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} dir
    93	 */
    94	export function cleanupTmp(fs, dir) {
    95	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
    96	  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    97	    if (/\.tmp\./.test(name)) {
    98	      try {
    99	        fs.unlinkSync(`${dir}/${name}`);
   100	      } catch {
   101	        // already gone or locked; not fatal
   102	      }
   103	    }
   104	  }
   105	}

+describe('hook.mjs — BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD fast-path no-op (l1-supervised-guard)', () => {
+  // Source of truth: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md
+  // §"Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3, finding 1)"
+  // + §"Acceptance constraints" constraint 1. When the supervisor sets
+  // BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD in a child's env, the hook entrypoint must fast-path
+  // no-op for EVERY event it handles WITHOUT spawning any baton child process:
+  // exit 0, empty stdout/stderr, and — critically — io.execFile is NEVER called
+  // (a spawned child would itself read/write the supervisor-owned bundle, which
+  // is exactly what the guard exists to prevent). Set unset/empty = normal.
+  const EVENTS = ['SessionStart', 'Stop', 'StopFailure', 'PreCompact', 'SessionEnd'];
+
+  it('every handled event fast-path no-ops without spawning ANY baton child', async () => {
+    for (const event of EVENTS) {
+      const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({ stdin: payload(event), files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(claudeBundle()) } });
+      io.env.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD = '1';
+
+      const code = await runHook([event], io);
+      assert.equal(code, 0, `${event}: supervised child exits 0`);
+      assert.equal(calls.length, 0, `${event}: NO baton child is spawned (io.execFile never called) under the guard`);
+      assert.equal(io.stdoutText(), '', `${event}: no stdout under the guard`);
+      assert.equal(io.stderrText(), '', `${event}: no stderr under the guard`);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('the guard beats StopFailure\'s double-spawn (neither checkpoint nor detect runs)', async () => {
+    const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({ stdin: payload('StopFailure', { error: { type: 'rate_limit' } }) });
+    io.env.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD = '1';
+    const code = await runHook(['StopFailure'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0);
+    assert.equal(findSub(calls, 'checkpoint').length, 0, 'no checkpoint child under the guard');
+    assert.equal(findSub(calls, 'detect').length, 0, 'no detect child under the guard');
+    assert.equal(calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned at all');
+  });
+
+  it('any non-empty value activates the fast-path (not just "1")', async () => {
+    const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({ stdin: payload('Stop') });
+    io.env.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD = 'yes';
+    assert.equal(await runHook(['Stop'], io), 0);
+    assert.equal(calls.length, 0, 'a non-"1" truthy value is equally a no-op');
+  });
+
+  it('NEGATIVE: empty-string BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD does NOT trigger the fast-path (a normal Stop still spawns a checkpoint)', async () => {
+    const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({ stdin: payload('Stop'), files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(claudeBundle()) } });
+    io.env.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD = '';
+    assert.equal(await runHook(['Stop'], io), 0);
+    assert.equal(findSub(calls, 'checkpoint').length, 1, 'empty-string env is NORMAL behavior: the checkpoint child still spawns');
+  });
+});
+

     6	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	// Gate-2 fix 8 (reviewer-a finding 11): Codex and Cursor SessionStart hooks
     8	// used to run FULL receive preparation — exit-1 noise with no bundle, a resume
     9	// prompt for the session's own open bundle, and the wrong output shape for
    10	// Cursor. `baton session-start` is the cheap gate the adapters call instead:
    11	// it inspects only pending-foreign state (sealed OR limit-hit, AND from
    12	// another platform), emits the harness-specific context shape, and ALWAYS
    13	// exits 0 — a session-start notice must never break a session.
    14	//
    15	// Pending matrix mirrors the claude-code hook contract (S3):
    16	//   positive:  foreign SEALED bundle                       -> context
    17	//   positive:  foreign OPEN + reasonClass usage-limit      -> context
    18	//   negative:  foreign OPEN non-limit                      -> nothing

    69	/** @param {string} dir */
    70	function walkFiles(dir) {
    71	  /** @type {string[]} */
    72	  const out = [];
    73	  if (!existsSync(dir)) return out;
    74	  for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
    75	    const p = join(dir, name);
    76	    if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) out.push(...walkFiles(p));
    77	    else out.push(p);
    78	  }
    79	  return out;
    80	}
    81	function frontmatter(md) {
    82	  const m = md.match(/^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---/);
    83	  return m ? m[1] : null;

     1	import { normSep, joinNorm } from '../util/pathnorm.mjs';
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
     5	 * @param {any} io
     6	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
     7	 */
     8	export function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
     9	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
    10	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
    11	}
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * Centralized usage-error emission (gate-2 fix 11): with --json the envelope
    15	 * contract holds even for bad invocations; otherwise a one-line stderr
    16	 * message. Always returns the usage exit code 2.
    17	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
    18	 * @param {string} cmd @param {string} msg
    19	 * @returns {number}
    20	 */
    21	export function usageError(io, flags, cmd, msg) {
    22	  if (flags.json === true) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg } });
    23	  else io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmd}: ${msg}\n`);
    24	  return 2;
    25	}
    26	
    27	/**
    28	 * Repo-root discovery (plan §Root discovery): `--root` wins; else the nearest
    29	 * ancestor of io.cwd carrying `.handoff/` or `baton.config.json`; else the
    30	 * nearest ancestor carrying `.git` (the repo toplevel — and a boundary the
    31	 * walk never crosses, so a nested repo resolves to itself); else io.cwd.
    32	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
    33	 * @returns {string}
    34	 */
    35	export function resolveRoot(io, flags) {
    36	  if (typeof flags.root === 'string' && flags.root.length > 0) return flags.root;
    37	  // Normalize separators up front (gate-2 iter-2 M2): a Windows cwd arrives
    38	  // backslash-separated, but Node fs accepts forward slashes, so the ascent
    39	  // and the marker checks work in one forward-slash space. The last separator
    40	  // is either kind; the loop stops at a drive/UNC or filesystem root.
    41	  const start = normSep(io.cwd);
    42	  let dir = start;
    43	  while (true) {
    44	    try {
    45	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.handoff')) || io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, 'baton.config.json'))) return dir;
    46	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.git'))) return dir; // repo toplevel; never walk past a .git boundary
    47	    } catch {
    48	      break;
    49	    }
    50	    const cut = dir.lastIndexOf('/');
    51	    if (cut < 0) break;
    52	    const parent = cut === 0 ? '/' : dir.slice(0, cut);
    53	    // At a drive-relative ceiling ("C:") examine the drive ROOT ("C:/") once —
    54	    // a repo can live directly at C:/ (iter-3 F6) — then stop.
    55	    if (/^[A-Za-z]:$/.test(parent)) {
    56	      const driveRoot = `${parent}/`;
    57	      if (driveRoot !== dir) {
    58	        dir = driveRoot;
    59	        continue;
    60	      }
    61	      break;
    62	    }
    63	    if (parent === dir) break;
    64	    dir = parent;
    65	  }
    66	  return start;
    67	}
    68	
    69	/** The supported platform enum, validated wherever a platform id is intake. */
    70	export const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * Validate a platform id against the enum (surface-audit fold): a typo'd
    74	 * platform used to silently seed persistent state whose bogus origin then
    75	 * rejected the REAL platform's checkpoints as foreign.
    76	 * @param {string | boolean | undefined} value
    77	 * @returns {string | null} an error message, or null when valid/absent
    78	 */
    79	export function platformError(value) {
    80	  if (value === undefined) return null;
    81	  if (typeof value === 'string' && PLATFORMS.includes(value)) return null;
    82	  return `platform must be one of ${PLATFORMS.join('|')} (got ${JSON.stringify(value)})`;
    83	}
    84	
    85	/**
    86	 * Strict flag parser (surface-audit fold; docs/design/core.md §tooling always
    87	 * specified strict parsing). spec maps each flag name to 'string' | 'boolean';
    88	 * `json` and `root` are implied common flags. Errors — unknown flag, stray
    89	 * positional, missing string-flag value — return {error} so the command exits
    90	 * 2 instead of silently losing intent: the lenient parser let a stray token
    91	 * flip SAFETY flags off (checkpoint/doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
    92	 * receive --print-prompt all read fail-dangerous exit 0).
    93	 * @param {string[]} args
    94	 * @param {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} spec
    95	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, error?: string}}
    96	 */
    97	export function parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) {
    98	  /** @type {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} */
    99	  const full = { json: 'boolean', root: 'string', ...spec };
   100	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
   101	  const flags = {};
   102	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
   103	    const a = args[i];
   104	    if (!a.startsWith('--')) return { flags, error: `unexpected argument '${a}' — flags are --name [value]` };
   105	    const key = a.slice(2);
   106	    const kind = full[key];
   107	    if (kind === undefined) return { flags, error: `unknown flag --${key}` };
   108	    if (kind === 'boolean') {
   109	      // A following token that is not a flag would have been this flag's
   110	      // silent "value" under the lenient parser; reject it loudly.
   111	      const next = args[i + 1];
   112	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
   113	        return { flags, error: `unexpected token '${next}' after --${key} (a bare flag takes no value)` };
   114	      }
   115	      flags[key] = true;
   116	      continue;
   117	    }
   118	    const next = args[i + 1];
   119	    // A string flag consumes the next token unless it is itself a KNOWN flag
   120	    // (so `--commit --origin x` errors instead of eating '--origin').
   121	    if (next === undefined || (next.startsWith('--') && full[next.slice(2)] !== undefined)) {
   122	      return { flags, error: `--${key} requires a value` };
   123	    }
   124	    flags[key] = next;
   125	    i += 1;
   126	  }
   127	  return { flags };
   128	}
   129

See [supervised-child-guard.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/supervised-child-guard.test.mjs:103), [shared.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/shared.mjs:45), [store.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/bundle/store.mjs:126), [memfs.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/helpers/memfs.mjs:147), [memfs.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/helpers/memfs.mjs:197). — Fix: extend the read spy to wrap `existsSync`, `readdirSync`, `statSync`, `lstatSync`, and `realpathSync` for `/repo/.handoff` and descendants, or narrow the documented assertion to content reads only.
2. [high] — The no-write spy is incomplete: the test comment claims every mutating call is covered, but the wrapper only records `writeFileSync`, `appendFileSync`, `mkdirSync`, and `renameSync`; fake fs also mutates via `unlinkSync`, `rmSync`, and `copyFileSync`, and core lock/store code uses `rmSync`/`unlinkSync`. See [supervised-child-guard.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/supervised-child-guard.test.mjs:94), [supervised-child-guard.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/supervised-child-guard.test.mjs:109), [memfs.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/helpers/memfs.mjs:167), [memfs.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/helpers/memfs.mjs:174), [memfs.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/helpers/memfs.mjs:222), [lock.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:214). — Fix: wrap all fake-fs mutators or assert `io.fs.__history.length === 0` in every guard-active command test, not only the first checkpoint case.
3. [medium] — The Claude hook guard tests prove “no spawn” but not “zero `.handoff` reads/writes”: the new hook block asserts `calls.length`, stdout, and stderr, but it does not instrument `io.fs`, so an inline-read or diagnostics-write implementation could silently pass while violating the plan. See [claude-code-hook-script.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/adapters/claude-code-hook-script.test.mjs:306), [claude-code-hook-script.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/adapters/claude-code-hook-script.test.mjs:313), [hook.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/adapters/claude-code/scripts/hook.mjs:60), [hook.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/adapters/claude-code/scripts/hook.mjs:86). — Fix: apply the same read/write/history spy to `runHook` guard-active cases and assert zero `.handoff` touches plus zero writes.
4. [medium] — The command guard tests only exercise `--platform claude-code`, while the acceptance constraint says hook commands on all three platforms must produce zero bundle writes and zero session-start resume context; Codex and Cursor templates invoke the same commands with `--platform codex` and `--platform cursor`. See [supervised-child-guard.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/supervised-child-guard.test.mjs:151), [supervised-child-guard.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/unit/supervised-child-guard.test.mjs:232), [goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:280), [codex/hooks.json](/Users/saksham/baton/adapters/codex/hooks.json:8), [cursor/hooks.json](/Users/saksham/baton/adapters/cursor/hooks.json:6). — Fix: table the guard-active `cmdCheckpoint` and `cmdSessionStart` tests over `claude-code`, `codex`, and `cursor`, including Cursor/Codex hook-shaped checkpoint args where relevant.
